And each person, thru persons he trusts, will get list of NON DUPLICATE Ids of some of 90 crore voters or all of 90 crore voters. Now all votes are on public ledger which can not be tempered by anyone. So every person can download all votes or some of the votes on his PC if he wants and also verify counts.
.
Possibly, Etherium too can be used for this task. One can make payment of Re 1 on Etheirium and put his vote as message. And those who wish to verify can collect all messages on blockchain. I dont know if ethroum allows all this. If not, I plan to write such blockchain code to have OPEN VOTING, but for next few months, I am too too busy with other tasks.
.

(3) Using twitter , fb .i.e. well known PRIVATE websites
.

A list of nationwide twitterids of 10 crore Indian voters (or more and more come on twitter) can be made by Govt or by private persons

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Jun 05 2018 06:14:22 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

comparing two proposals --- better along all dimensions vs "better along most dimensions only, and looking bad on some dimensions"
.
consider GST vs wealth tax. Then wealth tax is superior that GST along ALL dimensions. And so when a recallists is arguing against congress/rss/aap worker on GST vs WT topic before audience of say 1000 voters, the recallists will have easy time
.
But now consider judge system vs Jury System. JurySys is superior along many dimensions such as lack of nexuses, no fear of suspension, expulsion or unfavorable transfers, no enticement of promotion or favorable transfers etc etc. But on dimension, namely "KNOWLEDGE OF LAWS" , a congress/rss/aap worker can make JurySys look bad. Also, on other dimensions that such as temperament, biases, caste biases etc, they can make some noise.
.
Similarly, compare "weaponization of we the commons". The chances of mass slaughter by foreign armies and chances of large scale govt atrocities drastically reduce by weaponization of commons. But deaths due to random violence does increase with weaponization of commons.
.
IOW, a large number of recalist proposals are better than congress/rss/aap proposals along ALL dimensions. And so recallists can convince audience easily.
.
But in many cases, along one dimension, the recallist proposal can be made to look bad. So imo, what recallists will need to do is to make a LIST OF ALL dimensions / factors, and then show that along N dimensions, recallist proposal is better and along some useful or useless dimension, congress/rss/aap proposal may be better. Then one needs to give some SUBJECTIVE weightages to each dimension. And then show that recallist proposal is better when WEIGHTED SUM is taken. And finally, weightages are subjective. So solution I propose is "referendum for the proposed recallist law-draft" and not law-draft !!
.
eg on "weaponization of we the commons", my proposal is "REFERENDUM on proposed law-draft to enable all voters of India to bear guns". that way subjectivity of weights is decided by each voter himself. But GST vs WealthTax , I dont emphasis on word referendum, though eventually most laws we have proposed will come via newindia dot in or TCP only, which are similar to referendum.
.
So my point is --- it is important to first KNOW that along 1-2 dimensions, some recallist proposals can be made to look bad !! And in such cases, recallist should first make list of ALL dimensions, and then work to show that recallist proposal is better than congress/rss/aap proposals.

.

.

.

.

.
IOW, India is sitting on a valcano of possible large scale digital payment frauds.
.

.
(1) end use of OTP as conclusive proof
.

.
(4) all should be given SMS enabled landline at cheap rates

(5) OTP can be sent on landline too as an evidence but not proof.
.
False routing of SMS / call on landline is too too difficult

(1) say X supports a law-draft, but X says "I will NOT ask PM to print this law"!!. Then should A take X as "supporter"? IMO , NO
.
(2) say X supports a law-draft, but X says "I will surely ask PM to print this law, but NOT using most reliable channels"!!. Then should A take X as "supporter"? well, lets analyse
.
My stand on (1) is clear. I take a person as supporter of law-draft ONLY if that person "tells" PM to print that law-draft. He may have VALID reason to oppose / ignore law-draft Thats fine. I dont mind people who oppose proposed law-drafts. I only want to ask activists to stay away from FAKE supporters
.
Now say a person agrees to tell PM. Now there are 10s of methods to tell PM. Some are
.
(1) Take an aadhar link mobile phone of yours, and VOTE on newindia dot in --- when a person votes on newindia. PMO KNOWS (audience doesnt know, but PMO knows) that vote came from aadhar-linked mobile and not from some emailid or twitterid or fbid
.
(2) person votes on newindia using emailid, or fbid or twitterid
.
(3) person writes postcard
.
(4) person calls PMO's landline and gives message to staff
.
(5) person sends tweet to @PmoIndia .
.
(6) I will write on FB status "@PmoIndia , I support ...#HASHTAG etc etc"
.
(7) I will write "@PmoIndia #HASHTAG " on Ethereum
.
Now which is LEAST unreliable and least inexpensive method ?
.
IMO, least unreliable method is (1). Because when you call from PCO or mobile, the staff has to manually write down message and that can create typo-spelling error. Also, cost of keeping staff wiil be huge if lakhs of voters start calling. And calling leaves no record. Whereas on newindia, PMO as well as user (nut not thirdparty) can see which causes he has voted for
.
Likewise, when tweet is sent to @PmoIndia, the tweet reaches reliably, and inexpensively. But PMO has no way to know whether tweet sender is fake or real. Whereas on newindia dot in, if a person votes via his aadhar linked mobile, then PmoIndia knows that a real Indian voter is voting
.
And postcard is good if and only if person has no internet.
.
The plus point of tweet and fbposts is that your friends will come to know. So it has value in activism. But fbpost has zero value in communication to PM, and tweet has some value , but less than newindia dot in
.
Now each method has some plus points which other method doesnt. eg when I send tweet to @PmoIndia, my friends can see that I am supporter, but PmoIndia cant see that I am a real person. And when I vote on newindia dot in, PmoIndia can see that I am a real mobile using person or fake email using person, but my friends cant see.
.
So finally, I request ALL to use MULTIPLE methods ----
.
(1) vote on newindia dot in
.
(2) tweet PMOIndia.
.
(3) send postcard to PM
.
ALL of above 3 steps are must. If one step is missing, and I DO NOT take him as supporter of law-draft. Now course, if person has NO internet, then first two steps are NOT needed.

There are several parts in "proposed campaign method for a proposed law-draft"
.
Imo, two imp parts are
.
(1) method we ask voters to tell PM which proposed law-drafts that THAT voter supports
.
(2) methods voters / activists use to communicate proposals, communicate support, communicate opposition, weeding out fake supporters, VERIFY support, and get supporter-counts
.
(3) method to communicate to voters and AMUS = Ahimsamurti Mahatma Udham Singh that "PM is defying 45 crore voters". But this method is needed ONLY if PM is defying 45 crore voters. And if 45 crore voters have asked PM to print some law and PM is defying.
.
-------------
.
But first question is --- who is a law-draft SUPPORTER? Imo, a supporter is voter who has asked PM to print that law. If that supporter refuses to ask PM to print that law, then I request all NOT to count him as a supporter. Next, supporter should be also open to tell ALL that he supports that law-draft.
.
Now the mechanism we ask him to communicate to PM and communicate to ALL should be cost effective for voter, for PM and for all.
,.
eg imo we should NOT ask voter "I will take you as a supporter ONLY if you personally meet PM and hand him over this draft !!" It is NOT possible for 90 crore voters to travel all the way to Delhi to give a letter, not can PM meet 90 crore or even 9 lakh voters. So this is NOT a proper method,
.
Now there are countless methods --- going from sending postcards to slogan shouting to rallies to online petitions to what not. The method I propose are

(1) For voter to communicate to PM
.
(1a) vote for that proposal on newindia dot in USING AADHAR LINKED MOBILE and not using emailid . not using twitterid/fbid
.
(1b) send tweet to @PmoIndia
..
(1c) send postcard with name, voter# -- thats because only 10 crore have internet, and remaining 80 crore voters in India dont have internet
.

.

Following are tasks that can be automated using software
.
(A) if one has a file with million tweetlinks and tweets, one can retrieve all million tweets from twitter using tweetlinks
.
(B) if one has million ETHEREUM transaction-ids, one can retrieve all texts behind those ids.
.
--------------
.
So now can ask law--draft supporters to take following steps
.
(1) vote for that law on newindia dot in
.
(2) send tweet to @PmoIndia with hashtag
.
(3) put one or more tweetlink and tweettext BOTH in Ethereum Blockchain, along with previous transaction-id , And previous transaction-id will be nil if it is first transaction.
.
(4) Get new transaction-id and put in on twitter
.
-------------
.
Now how can activists get/prove list of supporters?
.
(1) assume that if a person has tweeted then he has voted . Later, if X personally meets Y, X can see on Y's mobile whether Y has voted or not
.
(2) Activists will need to make list of REAL twitterids thru information obtained on both --- OUTSIDE and on internet
.
(3) Activist will need to latest tweetlinks of these voters.
.
--------------
.
From latest tweetlinks, one can get latest .transaction-ids.
.
From transactionids, one can get all tweetlinks, and tweettexts,, and verify that tweettext are correct.
.
Thus one can know whether voters have actually sent tweets @PmoIndia or not.

California Voters Remove Aaron Persky, the Judge Who Gave a 6-Month Sentence for Sexual Assault
.
Aaron Persky, the California judge who drew national attention in 2016 when he sentenced a Stanford student to just six months in jail for sexually assaulting an unconscious woman, was recalled on Tuesday, according to The Associated Press. He is the first judge recalled in California in more than 80 years.

Judge Persky, 56, had served on the Santa Clara County Superior Court since 2003, and he began his most recent six-year term in June 2016. ....
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Jun 07 2018 09:18:46 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

FAQs about newindia dot in

.
One of the principal stand I have taken as of today is --- if you have NOT supported a law-draft on newindia dot in, I dont take you as supporter of THAT law-draft or proposal to govt (except in cases where person does NOT know how to use net all). And if you havent sent postcard to PM demanding that law-draft, then also I dont take you as supporter of that law-draft.(be netizen or non-netizen)
.
My stand may change if PmoIndia who own newindia dot in change functionality of newindia or he takes down newindia dot in. So my stand is wrt to today's newindia dot in
.
Newindia needs mobile or PC some rudimentary familiarity with net and needs skills to use OTP. Out of 90 crore voters, some 30 crore voters are capable of using it. And over 70 crore can send postcards.
.
So some FAQ about newindia dot in come. Here are my answers
.
---
.
(1) votecount on newindia is useless
Ans : imo, it is semi-useless, and not totally useless,. eg if a proposal has 50 votes, then it proves that less than 51 REAL voters in India supports it. But say votecount is 10 lakhs, Then all 10 lakh can be fake and so it doesnt prove that proposal has even one supporter, but it does prove that proposal has less than 10,00,001 REAL supporters . So votecount on newindia does prove UPPER BOUND of real-supporters.
.
-----
.
(2) PM has no way to know how many real voters voted YES and how many YES-sayers are fake
.
Ans : PM i.e. websiteowner does know, though people outside PMO dont. Because website stores whether vote came from mobile or SM. SM can be fake, but mobiles are now aadhar linked,. So duplicate votes -- i.e one person with 10 mobile but one aadhar, can be counted as one vote by PM.
.
Newindia dot in allows fake voting. But it DOES NOT mix fake votes and real votes like EVMs or ballot boxes.
.
---------
.
(3) Newindia dot in allows fake voting and so we should boycott it
.
Ans : sadly, NRIs who are Indian voters living outside India cant access newindia website using mobile phones because international SMS are expensive. So NRIs can access newindia only via SM account or emails. Hence, PM has valid reason to allow SM based logins,. Now PmoIndia could have shown votecount as two votecounts -- one from mobiles and second from SM accounts. But PmoIndia can justify absence of two separate votecounts as "oversight, work in progress etc". So PM has a valid reason to allow SM based logins.
.
Now say A is victim and B was witness. B says to A "I support you, and I will tell ALL that you were victimised. I will tell ALL at pan galla, all on char rasta, in all meetings, but I will NOT go to court and I will NOT become witness in court because judge is allowing fake people to become witness" !!!
.
I will dismiss as non reason. the judge can be corrupt, the judge may have already taken bribe and decided the judgement, the judge may be allowing fake wintnesses --- but all those are non-reason for B for NOT becoming witness for A
.
If B says A "I oppose you and I wont be your wintess in court" , then its fine. But if B says to A " I support you , I tell ALL everything,m but I wont be witness in court", , then B's stand should be dismissed as nonsense
.
So lets say "PmoIndia has kept SM logins on newindia dot so that fake votes can come". But if a person refuses to support your proposal on newindia dot in, then THAT reason is not the reason. There may be VALID reasons why he refuses to support that proposal. But presence of fake voters cant be so
.
------------
.
(4) why a person (a) who knows how to use newindia dot in (b) refuses to vote on newindia --- is NOT a supporter of that proposal?
.
Ans : If a person is a TRUE supporter of a law-draft, then THE FIRST thing he will do is tell PM and his MP that he supports the proposed law-draft. How can both be told? Newindia dot in is CHEAPEST and simplest way to RELIABLY send information to PmoIndia !! Next is twitter. And for non-netizen, postcard is only cost effective way. And so imo, ALL three MUST be used. So if a person has NOT used ALL three, then I dont take him as a supporter of that proposal.

.
----
.
Across times, across world, apex authorities have been X% chair occupiers and (100 - X)% decision makers i.e. there are some other people who take decisions, and the apex chair occupiers has to pretend that it is their own decision. The two questions are --
.
(a) what is this X number?
(b) and who are other persons , other than people of nation, who are taking decisions, and then convincing or forcing apex chair occupiers to pretend that these decisions are their own and not taken or imposed by others?
.
----
.
Now lets take present day USA.
.
In USA, at local levels, the elected officials such as elected District Police Chief, elected and/or recallable district judges etc are say X% decision makers and (100 - X%) chair occupiers. But they do make decisions at their levels. They are bound by State laws, Federal laws and also depend on the bankster elitemen to buy Municipal Bonds (without which the whole county's finance will collapse). Inside courts, the Jurors are completely immune to elitemen's pressure , but judges do cater to apex elitemen. But many judges are elected and recallable and so also depend on public's opinion.
.
The Jury System in state courts is weaker than in county courts and Jury System in Federal Court is now now extremely weak. And state judges are still elected and recallable, but federal judges are appointed by President and
.
In Federal Govt, decisions are taken by apex 2000 to 3000 elitemen (owners of bank holding companies, owners of Foundations etc). These elitemen are bankster elitemen, and landowning and mineral owning elitemen, who own much of the lands and minerals via Fraudations aka Foundations. Bansketers manufacture dollars from thin air and thus they earn these dollars or earn interest on it, And land owners earn money from capital gains or rents. And mineral mine owners earn from royalties. These elitemen buy mediamen using the dollars earned and thru mediamen, they influence voters, And thus thru mediamen, elitemen control all elected officials, including President.
.
But in USA, existing President is one the decision making elitemen himself. And many senators are also decision making elitemen. And all decision makers are Americans only (some British, but then us-elitemen also influence decisions in UK, and so there is balance).
.
IOW, in local govt in USA, the chair occupiers are also also decision makers. And in Central Govt, the decision makers are banksters and landowners, and also the chair-occupiers.
.
Now come to India.
.
In India, SoMoKe have reduced to mere chair occupiers and are no longer decision makers. Thats because all mediamen are own owned by elitemen, and SoMoKe are NOT elitemen who own even 0.01% of mediamen. Also, most judges are loyal to elitemen, and with flick of pen, they can make imprison all MLA-level or even MP-level leaders, and bring SoMoKe on their knees.
.
At local level, we have appointed officials and judges and none are elected. Jury System was killed by Indian elitemen long back. And all district judges are under iron grip of SCjs and locall local officers are under iron grip of state apex-IAS and central-apex-IAS. The apex-IAS are loyal to elitemen and not to any chair occupying political leader or people of India.
.
But the mess in India is now deep because the desi elitemen are too now chair occupiers and decision making powers are moving into the hands of usuk-elitemen !!
.
The reasons are --- poor weapon manufacturing and poor manufacturing of anything and everything in general. I will discuss that in my next post in this album ...

Reducing true activists' possible timewast ---- so just because some step is "good", doesnt mean that that step must be done , if there is a much better step because time, money and energy are limited. And which step is better --- it is SUBJECTIVE.
.
One action item in activism is to take steps that reduce TRUE activists' possible timewaste.
.
And it will be subjective. Examples to show subjectivity are ---
.
(a) an activist-A may think that "spending time on cause-X will be useful, spending time on cause-Y is time waste ". While activist-B may think other way
.
(b) an activist-A may think that "spending time on cause-X is useful, but law-draft-P is useful, while law-draft-Q is time waste". And activist-C may say "spending time on cause-X is useful, but law-draft-P is timewaste, while spending time on law-draft-Q is useful"
.
(c) an activist-C may think that "spending time on cause-X is useful, but law-draft-P is useful, while law-draft-Q is time waste". And activist-C may say "spending time on cause-X is useful, but law-draft-P is timewaste, while spending time on law-draft-Q is useful"
.
(d) and an activist-D may agree with cause, law-drafts of activist-A, but oppose the campaign steps , because he thinks that A's campaign steps are timewaste.
.
---
.
Some specific examples:
.
(1) I at right to recall party, and many recallists, support RTR, Jury etc law-drafts. while congress/rss/aap etc workers oppose these law-drafts because as per them, they are timewaste. and they consider SoMoKe-puja as geat activity and we consider it as timewaste.
,.
(2) within 'recallists", many consider some proposed law-drafts as good, and other proposed law-drafts as timewaste.
.
(3) and there is sharp division on campaign steps. eg I consider election contesting and newspaper advt as most effective while many consider these methods as useless. And many like myself consider newindia dot in, as highly useful, while some consider newindia dot in as highly timewaste.
.
----
,
So even if something is good, a person may not agree to do it, because he has VALID reasons from his point of view that something else is far far more time efficient. .
.
So each activist has to make a list of steps he wishes to take and NO take. And it will be subjective --- each one on his own.

.

.

.

.

.

.
working to ensure that "majority knows that majority supports proposed law-draft", is useful ONLY after majority has supported a proposed law-draft, or, number of supporters are more than supporters of leaders who oppose these law-drafts. eg NaMo has over 22 crore proven supporters or 25 crore proven supporters across India (RSS = BJP got some 22 crore votes in may-2014 elections, and NDA got some 25 crore votes). So unless RtrDeo3 has over 22 crore or over 25 crore supporters, I see no point in knowing "how many voters know that 33 voters support RtrDeo3"? .
.
So imo activists should work to inform all that N voters support proposed-law-draft-X after N is at least above sat 10 lakhs or say 1 crore. Till then working to inform "N support proposed-law-draft-X" is waste of 90 crore voters' time.

Now there are million proposals like RtrDeo3 . There are some 3000 on newindia dot in only. So if billion people of India decide to notice supporter-count of each of these million people, then they will end up spending whole of their 100 years' lifetime in just gathering this information !!
.
So as long as supporter-count for a proposed-maw-draft is extremely low, imo, it is waste of time for majority to know "how many voters support a proposed-law-draft".
.
working to ensure that "majority knows that majority supports proposed law-draft", is useful ONLY after majority has supported a proposed law-draft, or, number of supporters are more than supporters of leaders who oppose these law-drafts. eg NaMo has over 22 crore proven supporters or 25 crore proven supporters across India (RSS = BJP got some 22 crore votes in may-2014 elections, and NDA got some 25 crore votes). So unless RtrDeo3 has over 22 crore or over 25 crore supporters, I see no point in knowing "how many voters know that 33 voters support RtrDeo3"? .
.
So imo activists should work to inform all that N voters support proposed-law-draft-X after N is at least above sat 10 lakhs or say 1 crore. Till then working to inform "N support proposed-law-draft-X" is waste of 90 crore voters' time.

Proposed solution to temple breaking in Kashi and other cities --- across Kashi, Modiji / Yogiji and RSS-workers are demolishing many old small temples. SOLUTION?
.
If a road needs to be widened, and the temple (which was there before 1947 or was legally buit on legally purchased land after 1847) is on side, and then my proposal is

(1) govt should buy the plot right behind the temple, and
.
(2) the temple should be given THAT ADJACENT plot, so that temple doesnt need to relocate very far off.
.
If a road has to be made, then road has to be made. We cant shift the roads' locations. And if an existing road needs to be widened, then land on both or one side needs to be purchased at fair price, and ex-owner will need to relocate.
.
But what if the structure is old religious heritage structure?
.
And imo, govt should should give ADJACENT plot, by acquiring acquring adjacent plot rather than asking that temple to "relocate" to any distant place.
.
That way, temple will have to shift, but shift only by some 10-20-50 meters.
.
But from what I hear, Modiji / Yogiji and RSS-workers have started demolishing 100s of old small temples in kashi, and the temples are given small compensation that isnt enough to even buy same amount of land in the area where it is located. Sun Jun 10 2018 09:10:22 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Is this decision of Sri Modiji and RSS-workers dharmic, that "Govt shall levy Rs 5 lakh tax on someone who manufacturers Rs 1 crore of ghee making profit AFTER tax of no more than Rs 2 lakhs to Rs 4 lakhs, and Govt shall NOT levy even Re 1 of tax on plots of man who has Rs 500,000 crore of plots" ?
.
When discussing any proposed law, the blanket refusal from congress/aap workers is "we will let you know after our high command decides". But RSS-workers' refusal is quite "deep" and "spiritual" !! Their opening statement is "all laws ibncluding all tax laws should be based on dharma" !! And what is "dharma"? "dharma is not religion and .... " their goes another marathon speech on what is NOT dharmik and what is dharmic.
.
Now in science exams, there are some theory questions where one does copy-rote-paste of some textbook paragraphs. And sometimes there are numericals, where one need to APPLY principles stated in textbooks.
.
So here is a numerical.
.
(1) Modiji is Dharmic , because he is from RSS. I dont deny that.
.
(2) Modiji has printed law that if anyone makes and sells UNBRANDED ghee of above Rs 1 crore (say Rs 1.01 crore) then GST he has to pay is Rs 5 lakhs, Now input credit will be almost zero, because milk is GST exempt and fuel doesnt have GST input credit and neither does labor salaries. And GST on transport cannot be claimed as input credit. So TOTAL taxes he will pay will be Rs 5 lakhs minus zero input credit = Rs 5 lakhs.
.
Now profits --- after subtracting all material costs like milk, fuel and subtracting labor, transport, electricity etc and after subtracting GST of Rs 5 lakhs --- will NOT be more than Rs 3 to Rs 4 lakhs !!
.
(3) And same dharmic Modiji has printed law that those who have plots worth Rs 500,000 lakh crore (yes, no typo ; rupees five LAKH CRORE only, not rupees five crores) will NOT need to pay even Re 1 of wealth tax on those plots !! I am NOT cooking any data. Godrej owns 16 crore sqft in Mumbai alone which will be worth Rs 500,000 crore and he owns huge amount of plots across India, and Tata owns more minerals and plots than Godrej and Missionaries own more than plots than all Tatas, Godrej added. And wealth tax levied by Modiji on their plots is Re 0 !!!!
.
(4) And all RSS-workers I know support GST and oppose wealth tax i.e. they support Modiji;s decision NOT to levy wealth tax
.
----
.
So is this tax decision dharmic?
.
My answer is --- I will accept whatever answer RSS-workers give, Because RSS is currently largest group activists (over 40 lakh activists, with next largest group congress = 10 lakh activists and next comes cpi/cpm = 5 lakh activists and then aap = about 2 lakh activists) . So RSS workers shall decide what is dharmik.I will NOT follow / support their decision, but I will accept label "dharma" as they stamp.
.
But imo, everyone must know the answer of RSS-workers on what is dharmik, As per them, imposing 5% to 18% GST on gross sale difference (i.e. gross value added, or gross profits), and at the same time NOT levying even Re 1 of tax on mega plot owners is a very very dharmik decision. And levying tax on wealth above say 1500 sqft of land(and 3000 sqft of construction) per faimy is highly adhamik !!! I dont oppose their label of dharmik. But this is imo something all activists and voters should know..

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Jun 12 2018 04:11:11 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How GST will increase overcrowding in cities --- solution cancel GST and levy wealth tax. see last para
.
(excise/vat/servicetax/cst were killing business and so two alternatives were GST or wealth tax. SoMoke and congress/rss/aap workers decides to oppose wealth tax and instead use GST. Now GST has 10s of problems, and one is --- it will increase overcrowding in cities. I have explained here how).
.
Let me give one numerical example
.
(1) Say 100 persons in Mehasana some 75 km away from ahmd and all their customers are in ahmd. Say each is making ghee of say Rs 2 crore a year
.
(2) Say 100 persons in ahmedabad are making ghee of Rs 2 crore a year EACH and their customers are also in ahmd.
.
Now if a ghee manufacturers insists on paying GST then his customers may walk to GST evader
.
Now due to eWayBill system, it is difficult for Mehasana manufacturers to evade GST. They face risk of confiscation of transport vehicle and all goods, and serious fines. While eWayBill system law doesnt apply on manufacturers in Ahmadabad if customer is also in Ahmedabad
.
So Mehasana manufactures may decline to send goods without eWayBill in which case they will end up paying charging 5% more (GST on ghee is 5%).. Whereas Ahmedabad manufacturers, if buyer is also in ahmd, can easily evade GST because eWayBill law doesnt apply on him.
.
So many customers of Ahmedabad will switch to Ahmedabad manufacturers
.
So many ghee manufacturers will have to shift close to their sellers. Or in any business of the manufacturers in cities will grow and those outside cities will shrink.
-
----------------
.
Apply this on 100s of goods. GST is anywhere from 5% to 18% to 28%. Say a tiles manufacturer is in Morbi (a city in Gujarat which has highest tile manufacturing). And buyers are builders in Ahmedabad, GST on tiles is 28%, So if manufacturer is in ahmd, then GST evasion is easy, but if manufacturer is in Morbi, then GST evasion is difficult due to eWayBiill system.
.
Now most buyers in cities and many sellers are in cities and off city areas or small cities. So many manufacturers will try to shift to city. And so overcrowding in cities will increase
.
Now this wont apply to manufacturers where there is steep economy of scale.
.
All in all, GST law DEFACTO says
.
(a) if manufacturer and buyer are in same city , then evasion is GST easy because eWayBill system legally doesnt apply if both are in same city.
,.
(b) if manufacturer and buyer are in different city , then GST evasion is DIFFICULT because eWayBill system applies !!
.
So many buyers may switch to sellers in same city. Now buyers are more in cities and sellers are in off city areas or remote areas. So sellers in cities will grow and many sellers will try to shift to cities. And so crowding in cities will increase !!!
.
-------------
.
Solution I propose is to COMPLETELY scrap GST and levy wealth tax.
.
If you support the proposal of scrapping GST and levying wealth tax, there there is ONE 10-second step you can do TODAY to SUPPORT wealth tax --- pls vote at https://newindia.in/causes/WealthTax . To know more about WealthTax , pls see fb.com/10154511306361922 .
.
Pls note that congress/rss/aap workers across India have opposed wealth tax and supported GST. And SoGa / NaMo / ArKe too have opposed wealthtax and supported GST.
.
So if a person is congress/rss/aap worker or a SoMoKe-andhbhagat and he says that he opposes wealth tax and supports GST, then at least he is speaking truth. But if a person is a congress/rss/aap worker or a SoMoKe-andhbhagat, and he says that he supporters wealth tax and opposes GST, then I request all to dismiss him as a liar.

Bhaiyuji's maharaj's suicide and decision of Modiji, Devendra Ghazini and rss-workers' to loot temples in Maharashtra
.
Bhaiyyuji Maharaj , who committed suicide some 1-2 days back, was a spiritual leader. He had a large following in MH and MP.
.
He was only 50 years old, and was in perfect physical and mental health. He was an ace sportsman (and a modelling actor) in his youth, and had a daughter from first marriage. His first wife had passed away a few years back. And he had recetnly re-married some 1-2 years back. From what I read, his second wife had given birth to a baby boy some 3 - 4 months back. So if this be the case, a man has joy as well as reason to live. and a man feels that he has duty to live, no matter how harsh the conditions he is facing.
.
There were some domestic problems -- disputes between family members. But NO legal problems, they were only household problems. And who doesnt have such problems? People dont sucide due to such petty fights in family members. And he had NO financial problems --- all in family were well off. When one has problems amongst family members, but enough money, he can easily solve problems by buying separate flats or bunglaows for each family member. And he had no shartage of flats or bungalows. IOW, family dispute does NOT add to a reason as big as suicide.
.
One possible reason was ---- for past 10 years hBhaiyyuji Maharaj was bitterly fighting against congress's decision to loot temples. And he had supported Modiji, Devendra Fadnavis (now known as Devendra Ghazini) and RSS-workers across MH / MP , with hope that Modiji, Devendra Fadnavis and RSS-workers will end temple loot unleashed by congress leaders and congress workers.
.
Bhaiyuji Maharaj had personally worked to convince lakhs of his followers to vote for RSS = BJP, and convinced them that once rss-leaders and rss-workers come into positions, temple loot will end,.
.
But as we all know, Modiji, Devendra Ghazini (aka David Fadnavis) and RSS-workers are now more aggressive than congress/aap in temple looting across MH and India. With fear that Modiji may loose may-2019 elections, the loot has become more aggressive.
.
So Bhayujji had a huge loss of face before all his followers. His followers were upset against him.
.
For a man who is spiritual leader, loss oi face is more severe than financial loss. When there is financial loss, people at least dont doubt integrity. But when there is open breach of promises , people start doubting integrity of promise makers and some people will start suspecting and shouting against.
.
In my opinion, this was the principal reason that forced Bhaiyyji Maharaj to commit sucide.
.
Otherwise, domestic problems are non issues --- they come and go and for a man of this stature, they dont matter.
.
I have come across cases where people suicided due to severe financial loss, but never heard of case where a wealthy man committed suicide, that too when he had a daughter and also a three month old son !! No matter how depressed he is, the joy of newborn son will reduce the depression and moral duty to take care of newborn will force him to live against all suicide. So family reasons are not adding up.

जूरी का सार व फायदा.

. ^^^ ज्यूरी सिस्टम में दण्ड देने की शक्ति नागरिकों के हाथो में होती है जिससे राजा और उसके कर्मचारियों द्वारा कारोबारियों पर किये जा रहे नाजायज उत्पीड़न में कमी आती है, और उद्योगों के पनपने के अवसर कई गुना बढ़ जाते है।
.
इससे उद्योग धंधो का विकास होता है और तकनिकी विकास में तेजी आती है। तकीनीकी विकास के कारण हथियारों के निर्माण की प्रौद्योगिकी का विकास होता है, और आधुनिक हथियारों से राष्ट्र की सेना मजबूत हो जाती है। ग्रीस दुनिया का पहला देश था जिसमे ज्यूरी सिस्टम आया। और इसीलिए वे हथियारों की तकनीक में आगे निकल गए। सिकंदर को विश्व विजेता माना जाए या नहीं इस पर विवाद हो सकता है। लेकिन यह निर्विवाद है कि सिकंदर अपने काल में सर्वाधिक भूमि को जीतने में सफल हुआ था।
.
सिकंदर द्वारा 2200 साल पहले 18 फुट लम्बा भाला प्रयोग किया जा रहा था जबकि हम 500 साल पहले तक भी 6 फुट लम्बे भाले का प्रयोग कर रहे थे। ग्रीस ने ही सबसे पहले घोड़े की जीन का आविष्कार किया था जिससे उसके घुड़सवारों को घोड़े पर पकड़ मिली और वे अच्छे तरीके से लड़ पाये। सस्ता चमड़ा बनाने की तकनीक जुटाने के कारण सिकंदर के सभी सैनिक पूरी तरह चमड़े की पोशाक से ढके रहते थे। सिकंदर ले पास गुलेले थी जिनकी सहायता से सैंकड़ो किलोग्राम के पत्थरों को दुश्मन की सेना पर फेंका जा सकता था।
.
कुल मिलाकर सिकंदर के पास आधुनिक हथियारों का जखीरा था जिसने उसे इतने बड़े भू भाग को जीतने में सफल बनाया और इन हथियारो का निर्माण तकनिकी विकास के कारण सम्भव हुआ था। और यह तकनिकी विकास जूरी सिस्टम की देन थी। वक्त के साथ साथ ग्रीस में ज्यूरी सिस्टम कमजोर हो गया और ग्रीस का पतन हुआ।
.
ज्यूरी सिस्टम को अपनाने वाला अगला देश ब्रिटेन था। 12 शताब्दी में ब्रिटेन में ज्यूरी सिस्टम आया और 16 वी शताब्दी तक ब्रिटेन तकनिकी विकास के कारण दुनिया का सबसे ताकतवर देश बनने लगा था। उद्योगों के विकास के कारण व्रिटेन ने हथियारों की तकनीक जुटाई और आधुनिक हथियारों के कारण वे सबसे मजबूत सेना खड़ी करने में सफल हुए।
.
अमेरिका 1774 में ब्रिटेन से आजाद हुआ और उन्होंने अपने देश में ज्यूरी सिस्टम और राईट टू रिकॉल प्रक्रियाए लागू की। आज अमेरिका में सबसे मजबूत ज्यूरी सिस्टम और राइट टू रिकॉल प्रक्रियाएं है और यह अपवाद नहीं है कि अमरीका के पास दुनिया की सबसे ताकतवर फ़ौज और तकनीक है।
.
now what is one step you can do TODAY to increase possibility that Jury System can come in India? Pls vote at https://newindia.in/causes/JurySys3 .
.
अधिक जानकारी के लिए व समाधान यहाँ पर देखें
भारत के निचले कोर्ट में जूरी सिस्टम जल्दी और न्यायपूर्वक फैसलों के लिए ; Jury System in Lower Courts in India for quick and fair judgements ~ https://web.facebook.com/ProposedLawsHindi/posts/548841788627353

Why Missionaries hated Sant Sri Asaram Bapu? and why rss-workers hate Sant Sri Asaram Bapu?
.
(english translation is given later)
.
..... "अद्वेष्टा सर्वभूतानाम्" अर्थात किसी से द्वेष न रखो. सबके भले की भावना रखो लेकिन चतुर बनना, कोई तुम्हारे धर्म को हानि न पहुंचा दे...
आज कल तो क्या करते हैं कि आदिवासियों को पहाड़ी इलाकों में ले जाते हैं, ट्रक बंद-गाडी बंद कर देते हैं फिर बोलते हैं भगवान् का नाम लो, अम्बा माँ का नाम लो, विट्ठल का नाम लो, बोले गाडी को धक्का मारो. गाडी नहीं चलती है, क्योंकि ड्राईवर एक्सेलरेटर नहीं देता. फिर बोले ये सब भगवान् कच्चे व झूठे हैं, हमारा ईशु भगवान् सच्चा है. उसकी शरण जाओ. "हे ईशु भगवान्, हम तुमको मानते हैं" ऐसा बोलो और धक्का मारो.
उन बेचारे गरीब आदिवासियों को ऐसा गुलबुला के, भ्रमित करके और जब वो ऐसा बोलते हैं तो वो एक्सेलरेटर देता है और गाडी चलती है. अब बोलो ये धर्म है कि धतिंग है? सच बताओ तुमलोग.
ये आदिवासियों की सेवा है कि आदिवासियों का शोषण है तुम्ही बताओ भाई . नारायण हरि !! नारायण हरि !!
.
हमारे यहाँ सूरत में धर्मपुर डिस्ट्रिक्ट में ऐसे लोगों ने अस्पताल खोली, फिर जो लोग दवा लेने को आये तो उसको विटामिन-सी की गोली देवे. अब बुखार और विटामिन सी का कोई सम्बन्ध नहीं है !! फिर बोले अम्बा माँ का नाम ले के खाओ, कृष्ण का नाम ले के खाओ. हमारे सब देवी देवताओं का नाम ले के उसको दवाई रोज रोज देवे, कुछ नहीं हॉवे. वो बोले- "देखो, तुम्हारा देव में कोई दम नहीं है, अब हमारा ईशु भगवान को मानो, ईशु भगवान की दया होगी तो तुम ठीक हो जाओगे. आज इस्शु भगवान् को प्रार्थना करो.
.
तो फिर ईशु भगवान् को प्रार्थना कराके, फिर उसको कुनैन का इंजेक्शन देते हैं और बुखार की गोली देते हैं, उसको ठीक हो जाता है. अब ठीक तो गोली से होता है और भूत घुसेड देते हैं धर्मान्तर का. कैसा जुल्म हो रहा है अपने देश में. कितना जुल्म हो रहा है इस इक्कीसवे सदी में. कितना जुलम कहूँगा, बीस सौ हो गए. ये अच्छा है क्या? अपने अपने इलाकों में सावधान रहो.
.
हिन्दू भाइयों को, बहनों को लोग विधर्मी बना दे फिर उनके बच्चे तुम्हारे दुश्मन हो जायेंगे.
.

.

Narayana Hari !!

आज कल तो क्या करते हैं कि आदिवासियों को पहाड़ी इलाकों में ले जाते हैं, ट्रक बंद-गाडी बंद कर देते हैं फिर बोलते हैं भगवान् का नाम लो, अम्बा माँ का नाम लो, विट्ठल का नाम लो, बोले गाडी को धक्का मारो. गाडी नहीं चलती है, क्योंकि ड्राईवर एक्सेलरेटर नहीं देता. फिर बोले ये सब भगवान् कच्चे व झूठे हैं, हमारा ईशु भगवान् सच्चा है. उसकी शरण जाओ. "हे ईशु भगवान्, हम तुमको मानते हैं" ऐसा बोलो और धक्का मारो.
उन बेचारे गरीब आदिवासियों को ऐसा गुलबुला के, भ्रमित करके और जब वो ऐसा बोलते हैं तो वो एक्सेलरेटर देता है और गाडी चलती है. अब बोलो ये धर्म है कि धतिंग है? सच बताओ तुमलोग.
ये आदिवासियों की सेवा है कि आदिवासियों का शोषण है तुम्ही बताओ भाई . नारायण हरि !! नारायण हरि !!
.
हमारे यहाँ सूरत में धर्मपुर डिस्ट्रिक्ट में ऐसे लोगों ने अस्पताल खोली, फिर जो लोग दवा लेने को आये तो उसको विटामिन-सी की गोली देवे. अब बुखार और विटामिन सी का कोई सम्बन्ध नहीं है !! फिर बोले अम्बा माँ का नाम ले के खाओ, कृष्ण का नाम ले के खाओ. हमारे सब देवी देवताओं का नाम ले के उसको दवाई रोज रोज देवे, कुछ नहीं हॉवे. वो बोले- "देखो, तुम्हारा देव में कोई दम नहीं है, अब हमारा ईशु भगवान को मानो, ईशु भगवान की दया होगी तो तुम ठीक हो जाओगे. आज इस्शु भगवान् को प्रार्थना करो.
.
तो फिर ईशु भगवान् को प्रार्थना कराके, फिर उसको कुनैन का इंजेक्शन देते हैं और बुखार की गोली देते हैं, उसको ठीक हो जाता है. अब ठीक तो गोली से होता है और भूत घुसेड देते हैं धर्मान्तर का. कैसा जुल्म हो रहा है अपने देश में. कितना जुल्म हो रहा है इस इक्कीसवे सदी में. कितना जुलम कहूँगा, बीस सौ हो गए. ये अच्छा है क्या? अपने अपने इलाकों में सावधान रहो.
.

And in year 2013 and year 2014, Mr. Rajesh Jain was an apex campaign manager for Sri Modiji.. I dont oppose his working for Duratma Anna or Kalki Purush Modiji --- he provided IT services to them just as many IT persons do.
.
Now since feb-2018, Mr. Rajesh Jain is campaigning for "Dhan Vapasi" nonsense proposal.
.
So what is this "Dhan Vapasi" nonsense proposal? And how does it ruin good proposals like MRCM? And how can we activists possibly save MRCM-law-draft from this Dhan Vapasi nonsense?
.
Mr. Rajesh Jain in his epic "Dhan Vapasi" nonsense proposal says "Govt shall give Rs 1 lakh every year to each and every of the 26 crore families in India": !!! Gee man, where will Rs 26 lakh crore a year come from? He says "the money will come from renting govt plots and from mineral royalties". So a part of Dhan Vapasi proposal looks like MRCM-law-draft I have proposed. In MRCM-law-draft too, 65% of mineral royalties and govt land rent goes to 135 crore commons equally. But in MRCM, there is NO nonsense promise like "every family will get Rs 100,000 every year". Because land prices, land rents, mineral royalties fluctuate every year and land rent and land prices will go 1/10th after MRCM (and WealthTax) comes.
.
So imo Dhan Vapasi is royal piece of nonsense proposal, while MRCM-law-draft is good proposal.
.
So how can Dhan Vapasi nonsense damage MRCM-law-draft proposal?
.
Say there are 90 crore voters in India and some 20 lakh activists in India.
.
(1) Now say a voter or activist first reads about MRCM-law-draft and then comes to know about about Dhan Vapasi nonsense. Then he will see that Dhan Vapasi is a nonsense clone and and he will continue to support MRCM-law-draft
.
(2) But consider a voter/activist who has never heard of MRCM-law-draft. Say he FIRST hears about Dhan Vapasi nonsense. Now
.
(2a) he may become supporter of Dhab Vapasi OR
(2b) realize that Dhan Vapasi is a nonsense OR
(2c) he may support Dhan Vapasi in first , and later realize that it is nonsense
.
Now in all three cases, chances that he will oppose MRCM later increases !!!
.
WHY?
.
Because moment voter or activist in (2a) or (2b) or (2c) reads about MRCM-law-draft, and see that MRCM-law-draft talks about "distribution of mineral royalty and land rent from govt plots" and will immidiately equate MRCM with Dhan Vapasi. And so he will immidtaley take MRCM has Dhan Vapasi nonsense clone and so oppose MRCM-law-draft as clone or nonsense !!
.
So by promoting a bad proposal, which looks like a good proposal but is different, elitemen can drastically lower the rate at which a good proposal can spread.
.

.

.
Lets say a person knows MRCM-law-draft with details. In that case, no matter how much he sees about Dhan Vapasi nonsense, he will never get impressed by Dhan Vapasi and in his eyes, reputation of MRCM-law-draft will NOT decrease.
.

.

If a person knows RTR-MP-law-draft as proposed by myself at RRP first and then he reads Varun Gandhi's RTR-MP-law-draft, then he will dismiss Varun Gandhi's draft as nonsense and reputation of RRP's RTR-MP-law-draft will NOT decrease. But if a person first reads Varun Gandhi's nonsense RTR-MP-law-draft, then he will get a feeling that RTR is a nonsense concept, and so later he is very likely to even refuse to read RRP's RTR-MP-law-draft.
.
So whats the solution?
.
First, this shows why drafts are very important, far far more important than we think or I thought. The reason I Took "draft first" approach way back in 1995, was to ensure that reader gets full details of the CONCEPTS as well as how drafts actually and do implement these concepts. But it turns out DRAFTS are needed also --- to stop elitemen from maligning the concepts using fake drafts !! IOW, in absence of good drafts, a good concept can be shown as bad concept using a bad draft !! So the "draft first" approach I took 1995 is more right that I thought !!
.
Second, imo, we should see that spreading DRAFT is more important than anything else !!! So translation in local languages and distribution of pamphlets , PDFs etc are imp
.
And third --- writing drafts on 100s of issues and concepts !!! More the issues we have have, more difficult it will be for elitemen and psuedo-activists to malign these concepts. eg Mr. Rajesh Jain has Dhan Vapasi nonsense proposal to malign MRCM. So we can question him on how he plans to fix courtsm how he plans to reduce problems of factory owners, what alternative does he propose to GST's missing trader problem and "double taxation on small trader's problem" and so on. Using this, we can convince activists that he proposals are too incomplete to fix the govt. And once we get ear of the activists, we can convince him why Dhan Vapasi is nonsense and why MRCM-law-draft is good !!
.
So consider an activist who believes Dhan Vapasi is good. So if we talk about MRCM-law-draft, he wont listen to us. But if we talk about fixing labor courts or courts or Bangladeshi problem, then he will listen to us because Rajesh Jain is big zero on these issues. So once that activist listens to us, we can also convince him that Dhan Vapasi is nonsense and MRCM-law-draft is good
.
IOW, "100s of law-draft" is more useful than we think. Many times, I get suggestions that "one should focus on 2-3 issues" whereas from day one, I considered this suggestion useless. "If there are 100 problems, then how can any problem be ignored or wished away" was my counter argument. It may be good politics to focus on 2-3 issues and ignore other 98, but it is surely bad activism. Now again, it turns out "having 500 drafts to solve 100 problems": is far better than we though. If elitemen try to malign one good draft using one bad draft, then other 99 good drafts can be used to to protect that law-draft !!!
.
So resource is one issue that goes against us and goes HEAVILY in there favor. But question before us is --- what can we do with resources we have i.e. money we have and people we have/
.
IMO, best option is to
.

(2) translation of drafts in as many languages as possible
(3) inform people about links of dragts, text of drafts and summary of drafts
(4) pamphlets , whatsapp, FB etc
(5) contest election, independent or using party names --- ask all candidates to put newindia dot in links and sha1 hash of the drafts in the affidavits
.
So our campaign has be is draft centric from day-1, and it turns out that drafts are more important than I or any or us thought !! But earlier we FIRST inform voters about drafts, better.

And solition is --- we spread information about our DRAFT as fast as we can , and WE REACH FIRST.

How free Military cover USA gives to Iceland helps Iceland, but for how long?
.
Iceland is a very small nation. It has a population of slightly over 300,000 people, fewer than many cities of India .
.
They forced a draw against Argentina, with Lionel Messi hitting 11 shots (including a penalty) but not getting a goal.
.
What is more interesting is that 5 of the players in the squad are Medical doctors. Everyone in the team has a professional job they do. The goalkeeper is a renowned country's Movie Director and others in different respectable professions.
.
Even the Coach when he is off the field is a practicing Dentist.
.
===================
.
People in iceland are happy and productive because they have to allocate zero resources / population for weapon manufacturing and running a REAL military. Thats because they are protected by USA = NATO for free. Why is USA protecting Iceland for free? And when USA may end this protection and instead decide to loot / destroy Iceland? The way USA ended protection over Iraq and decided to loot/destroy Iraq? And many african/latin countries? And the way France decided to end cover it had given to Libya and instead decided to join USA in looting / destroying Libya? Each one can speculate on his own.
.
But when a country is free from burden of weapon manufacturing and running a REAL military, many superficial good happy things happens. And they last as long as free defense cover lasts. The day free defense cover ends, the country becomes a hell hole. And often, the dominant conutry which first provided free defense cover is the same country that becomes the predator. There are countless examples in history. History may or may not repeat for Iceland, and other freeloaders like Canada, Germany, Spain, Portugal etc.
.
------------
.
in absence of free defence cover, a small country has no option but to merge with large country and then problems that come with size come. So happiness iceland has is due to small size, but "protection despite small size" is because USA is protecting it for free. One cant say how long such happiness would last.

The most important problem in activist --- betrayal by timepass by new elected MP. And solution I propose is --- "from day-one, activists should promote DRAFTS and candidates whose manifesto or party manifesto and/or affidavits have these drafts". So how do drafts help?
.
summary
------------
.
Imo, the most important problem in politics is that newly elected MPs do timepass to implement their promises by giving reasons after reasons for delay. These MPs will NOT outrightly refuse, But they will delay and delay and never leep the promises. There are countless examples in past and recent past.
.
Solution I propose is --- (a) from day-1 activists should promote DRAFTS (b) then activists should promote ONLY those election candidates who or whose parties or both have supported these drafts. (c) and if possible, the support for draft should be in concrete form such as citing newindia dot in links and sha1hash in manifesto, election affidavits etc
.
How does these draft reduce the problem of betrayal?
.
Consider two parties - say RRP or any party such as congress/rss/aap/others. Say all parties have promises RTR-SupremeCourt-judge law but only RRP has given draft.
.
Now in which case, possibility of not keeping promise is LESSER? and why?
.
The congress/rss/aap/other candidates gave promise for some laws, but didnt give DRAFTS. So after election, they can form committees for drafts and delay and delay. They will never refuse, they will simply delay.
.
But in case of RRP elected members, drafts are given before elections, and so they cant delay in the name of draft writing. So if they want to default, only option they have is to outrightly refuse.
.
So RRP elected candidates delay, then Ahimsamurti Mahatma Udham Singh can act.
.
So why can Ahimsamurti Udham Singh can act on RRP MPs and not on congress/rss/aap/other MPs?
.
Because one IMPORTANT pre-condition for AMUS to act is --- OBJECTIVE breach of OBJECTIVE promise . If the promise is vague, then there is no breach and certainly no objective breach.
.
IOW, force of AMUS is applicable on RRP MPs, while force of AMUS isnt applicable on congress/rss/aap/others MPs.
.
So imo, chances that activists will suffer betrayal are less if they support election candidates who have promised drafts in their manifesto or in their party manifesto.
.
And what can be FORMAT of promise? Imo, best format is newindia dot in links in manifesto along with sha1hash. Along with manifesto, it can also be in their election affidavit.
.
Now can other parties adopt our methods? NO. because congress/rss/aap/other parties have flatly refused to give DRAFTS. You mention LAW-DRAFTS and they immidiately insist on delay !! So AMUS's counter gurantee will not apply on them.
.
So whats the solution to problem of "breach of promises by newly elected MPs"? Solution I propose to REDUCE the possibility of breach is -- from day-1 , before election, activists should publicize drafts and then support only those party/candidates who have supported drafts on their manifesto
.
So, my MOST IMPORTANT point is
-------------------------------------
.
In my proposed solution, the activists have to act BEFORE the betrayal problem comes. I repeat --- the activists must act BEFORE the problem of betray comes. My solution is NOT that --- "if new MPs start betrayal, then after activists should next start publicizing law-drafts". The solution starts BEFORE problem comes !!! The activists must publicize law-drafts BEFORE betrayal or fulfillment comes. "Before, and not after", is one important point in method I suggested

https://timesofindia.indiatimes.com/city/mumbai/maharashtra-govt-to-take-control-of-shani-shingnapur-temple/articleshow/64673350.cms
.
Maharashtra govt to take control of Shani Shingnapur temple
.
21-jun-2018
.
MUMBAI: The state cabinet has approved a proposal to take over control of Shani Shingnapur temple in Ahmednagar district.
.
This came after an agitation by activist Trupti Desai two years back. The state will frame an Act for management of the temple. A bill will be table before the monsoon session, scheduled to commence on July 4 in Nagpur.
Desai welcomed the move but demanded 50% trustees and a priest should be women. The cabinet decision also came after several complaints about irregularities and corruption in the management system and process of appointment of committees.
.
Desai said it was a victory of her protest started in January 2016. She added that they plan to meet chief minister Devendra Fadnavis next week to demand appointment of female trustees. For over four centuries, women had been barred from stepping on to a high platform on which stands a black stone-symbolizing Lord Shanidev, the personification of planet Saturn. However, the temple trust in April 2016 took a decision to end the age-old discriminatory practice and allow women inside the sanctum sanctorum. The Maharashtra government had said that it will not allow any discrimination between men and women devotees.
.
============== end of ToI article ===========
.
Pls see how temple takeover is linked with egalitarion cause of women empowerment !!!

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Thu Jun 28 2018 08:12:05 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

देवी इंदिरा गांधी तकनीकी रूप से तानाशाह नही थी ; लेकिन वे पूर्ण लोकतंत्र की भी पक्षधर नही थी ।
.
भारतीय संविधान चुनकर आए प्रधानमंत्री को यह संवैधानिक शक्ति देता है कि वह आपात क़ालीन परिस्थितियों में इमर्जेंसी लगा सके । इंदिरा जी चुनकर आयी थी और उन्होंने अपनी संवैधानिक शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए इमर्जेंसी लगायी थी । अतः वह एक कठोर फ़ैसला था , किंतु तानाशाह फ़ैसला नही था ।
.
यदि कल भारत का जनरल फ़ौजों के बल पर पीएम को जेल में डालकर टेक ओवर कर लेता है तो वह तानाशाही होगी । क्योंकि यह संविधान सम्मत नही है ।
.
दूसरी बात - जब भारत की जनता ने देखा कि उन्होंने ग़द्दारों को सत्ता पकड़ा दी है , तो 1979 में उन्होंने फिर से इंदिरा जी को चुना था । इस लिहाज़ से जनता ने उन्हें इमर्जेंसी के लिए माफ़ कर दिया था । वे फिर से लोकतांत्रिक तरीक़े से चुन कर सत्ता में लौट आयी ।
.
मैं आपातकाल के फ़ैसले को सही नही मानता । लेकिन इंदिरा जी जूरी सिस्टम और राईट टू रिकॉल क़ानूनो की विरोधी थी , और उस समय जो हालात थे उनसे सिर्फ़ इन कानूनो को लागू करके ही निपटा जा सकता था । और यहीं पर उन्होंने जीवन की सबसे बड़ी भूल की । यदि वे देश जूरी सिस्टम और राईट टू रिकॉल पी एम का क़ानून डाल देती तो ख़ुद को इमर्जेंसी लगाने से बचा सकती थी ।
.

यदि देवी इंदिरा गांधी जजों को कंट्रोल करने में सफल हो जाती तो उन्हें इमर्जेंसी का फ़ैसला नही लेना पड़ता ।
.
इलाहाबाद हाई कोर्ट के भ्रष्ट जज जगमोहन लाल सिन्हा ने जब इंदिरा जी को अदालत में बुलाया तो , १० मिनिट पहले यह आदेश जारी कर दिया था कि यदि इंदिरा जी के आने पर कोई व्यक्ति अपनी कुर्सी छोड़कर खड़ा होगा तो इसे अदालत की अवमानना माना जाएगा । सिन्हा ने एक फ़र्ज़ी मुक़दमे का सहारा लेकर इंदिरा जी के अगले 6 वर्ष तक चुनाव लड़ने पर रोक लगा दी थी । ऐसे हालात में इंदिरा जी के पास सिर्फ़ दो रास्ते थे ;
.
1. वे राईट टू रिकॉल जज , राईट टू रिकॉल पी एम और जूरी सिस्टम लागू करे , या
2. इमर्जेंसी लगाए
.
वे राईट टू रिकॉल / जूरी सिस्टम के ख़िलाफ़ थी, अतः इमर्जेंसी के सिवाय उनके पास कोई मार्ग नही बचा था । यदि वे इमर्जेंसी न लगाती तो हालात बदतर हो जाते । इसकी तस्दीक़ जनता सरकार के शासन काल से की जा सकती है ।
.
परमाणु परीक्षण और पाकिस्तान के टुकड़े कर देने की वजह से अमेरिका और रूस इंदिरा जी से नाराज़ हो गए थे और उन्हें अपदस्थ करना चाहते थे । और इसके लिए उन्होंने भारत के भ्रष्ट जजों का इस्तेमाल किया ।
.
दरअसल भ्रष्ट जजों को जूरी सिस्टम और राईट टू रिकॉल के अलावा किसी भी तरह से क़ाबू में नही किया जा सकता । किसी भी तरह से नही । लेकिन इंदिरा जी जूरी सिस्टम / राईट टू रिकॉल कानूनो के ख़िलाफ़ थी । ऐसे हालात में इमर्जेंसी एक मात्र विकल्प था ।

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sat Jun 30 2018 09:57:32 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

मन्दसौर रेप केस ; समाधान — मंदसौर जिले की मतदाता सूची में से 25-55 वर्ष तक की आयु के बीच के 50 नागरिकों की ज्यूरी बुलाई जाए । यह ज्यूरी इस मामले की नियमित सुनवाई करके आरोपी को सज़ा देगी । ज्यूरी चाहे तो आरोपी का सार्वजनिक नार्को टेस्ट लेकर यह पता लगा सकती है कि क्या आरोपी पूर्व में भी इस तरह के यौन अपराध में लिप्त रह चुके है । ज्यूरी आरोप सिद्ध होने पर अभियुक्तों को फाँसी दे सकती है ।
.
इस तरह इस मामले का निपटान हफ़्ते दो हफ़्ते में हो जाएगा , और कठोर सज़ा मिलने से अन्य अपराधी हतोत्साहित होंगे ।
.
ज्यूरी के गठन एवं संचालन से सम्बंधित प्रस्तावित प्रक्रिया का ड्राफ़्ट मेरी फ़ेसबुक प्रोफ़ायल के कवर पेज को क्लिक करके देखा जा सकता है ।
.
सोनिया-केजरीवाल एवं इनके अंध भगत आरोपीयों की ज्यूरी द्वारा सुनवाई किए जाने का विरोध कर रहे है । लेकिन उनका यह विरोध महत्त्वहीन है । क्योंकि यदि मोदी साहेब चाहे तो ज्यूरी ट्रायल का आदेश कर सकते है । लेकिन संघ के सभी कार्यकर्ता एवं मोदी साहेब नही चाहते कि अभियुक्तों को त्वरित रूप से कठोर सज़ा मिले । वे इसका साम्प्रदायिक इस्तेमाल करके भावनाएँ भड़काना चाहते है । क्योंकि इससे संघ का वोट बैंक बढ़ता है । इसीलिए संघ के कार्यकर्ता एवं मोदी साहेब भी अभियुक्तों के सार्वजनिक नार्को टेस्ट एवं ज्यूरी ट्रायल का विरोध कर रहे है ।
.

(a) Modiji's "drama" of stopping export of alive animals (b) why Jains demand was wrong (c) how Modiji will use to increase animosity against Jains in many Hindus
.
First, Modiji issued orders to organize a grand event --- some 1 lakh goats/sheep will be put on a huge cargo plane in Nagpur, and sent to Saudi Arabia for slaughter !! And to show it as a grand achievement, Modiji asked 4 apex RSS-ministers to be present at cargo airport , and celebrate as it it was some major festive event and a major govt achievement !!! Newspaper advt were given across India to publicize this. Almost ALL 40 lakh RSS-workers had supported this "event". (if you have proofs that N out of 40 lakhs RSS workers opposed this live goat export, pls post the evidences on your wall first, and then here in comment).
.
The Jain community got aggravated. And all they should have demanded was ending subsidies to meat export and subsidies to export of live animals. But instead, Jain demanded complete ban on the export of live animals !! After some protest, Modiji cancelled the event.
.
Now export of alive animals to Saudi Arabia, China etc will go on. Only this INAUGURATION event was cancelled.
.
Now Modiji and RSS-workers are telling Rabaries etc that "see, you cnat get good price of your goats etc because Jains are blocking the export of goats" !! IOW, Modiji and RSS-workers will use this to increase animosity between Jains and many goat raising Hindu groups.
.
And pls note --- ONLY inaugaration event has been called off. The export of goats/sheet to Saudi Arabia, China, Vietnam etc will go on
.
Now what Jains demanded was also wrong. Imo, they should have confined the demand to ending subsidies to meat export and export of alive animals.
.
So Modiji and RSS-workers achieved several things with just one show. The inaugaration event has been called off, but export of goats etc will go on. And they have increased animosity between Jains and many goat raising Hindu groups.
